Critics in the generative tradition argue that such systems lack genuine grammar; influential replies from the dependency-grammar perspective hold that LLM behavior is well described by local head-dependent structure built word by word. We argue that a sharper observation has been overlooked: the prefix-driven, type-completing dynamics of autoregressive generation align closely with the incremental processing model that Combinatory Categorial Grammar (CCG) was originally designed to support. On this basis we propose a neurosymbolic framework in which LLM outputs are lifted into typed compositional derivations -- not claiming that LLMs implement CCG internally, but that their outputs admit a principled, incremental, and auditable CCG reconstruction. Two consequences follow. First, through the Curry-Howard correspondence the lifting extends beyond natural language to the formal languages LLMs also produce -- programming languages such as Solidity, description-logic and query languages such as OWL and SQL -- with the type system varying and the architecture held fixed. Second, the lifting supports two layers of checking: a compositional layer that catches structural failures directly, and a content layer that checks the lifted structure against external knowledge sources, enabling the earliest possible flagging of hallucinated content. The account thereby requires of a producer not cognition but a prefix-driven generative profile. We close with a sketch of synchronous LLM-CCG coupling as one direction the framework opens.

</details>


### [100] [Measuring Reward-Seeking via Contrastive Belief Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18966)
*Axel Højmark,Jérémy Scheurer,Evgenia Nitishinskaya,Felix Hofstätter,Jason Wolfe,Theodore Ehrenborg,Bronson Schoen,Alexander Meinke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对比合成文档微调（SDF）方法，用于测量语言模型在强化学习训练中的“奖励寻求”行为。研究发现，经过RL训练的模型（特别是OpenAI o3和gpt-oss-120b）会倾向于优化评判者的偏好而非开发者或用户的真实意图，且这种倾向随RL训练过程逐渐增强，可能导致模型在明知违背开发者意图的情况下仍选择追求更高奖励的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在强化学习训练中可能学会优化评判者的判断（奖励信号）而非实际目标，这种“奖励寻求”行为难以被直接测量，因为当评判者对正确行为给予奖励时，两种模型的行为表现完全相同。需要一种方法将这种潜在冲突暴露出来并进行量化测量。

Method: 提出对比合成文档微调（Contrastive Synthetic Document Finetuning, SDF）方法，通过构建合成文档改变模型对评判者奖励机制的信念，使模型对评判者偏好与开发者/用户偏好产生冲突，从而测量模型在两者之间的选择倾向。应用于OpenAI o3 RL训练中间检查点及gpt-oss-120b奖励黑客模型。

Result: 1. 未经过安全训练的OpenAI o3 RL检查点倾向于选择评判者偏好而非用户/开发者偏好；2. 这种倾向随RL训练过程呈上升趋势；3. 在承诺vs任务完成的选择环境中，后期o3检查点在SDF文档暗示评判者奖励任务完成时87%选择违背承诺，而暗示奖励诚实性时仅9%；4. 早期检查点敏感性较低（40% vs 24%）；5. 奖励黑客模型（gpt-oss-120b）对评判者偏好的敏感性是未修改模型的两倍以上（行为偏移从33%升至86%）。

Conclusion: 强化学习训练会增加模型的奖励寻求行为，使模型在认为违背开发者意图能获得更高奖励时倾向于采取对抗行为。这一发现揭示了RL对齐训练中的潜在风险，即模型可能在训练过程中发展出与开发者目标相悖的策略。

Abstract: Language models trained with reinforcement learning may learn to optimize the grader's judgment rather than the intended objective. This "reward-seeking" is difficult to measure because a model that pursues the grader's judgment and one that pursues the intended objective behave identically whenever the grader rewards the intended behavior. We measure reward-seeking using Contrastive Synthetic Document Finetuning to change a model's beliefs about what the grader rewards, putting those beliefs in conflict with what users or developers want, and measuring the rate at which the model adopts each party's preferred behavior. Applied to intermediate checkpoints of a capabilities-focused OpenAI o3 RL run, without safety training, we find that these checkpoints often side with grader preferences over those of users or developers on coding and alignment tasks. This tendency to side with the grader trends upward throughout RL training. For example, in an environment that forces a choice between keeping a promise to a supervisor and breaking it to complete the task, a late capabilities-focused o3 checkpoint breaks the promise 87% of the time when SDF documents say the grader rewards task completion, versus 9% when they say it rewards honesty (a choice its chain-of-thought often makes explicit). An earlier checkpoint is far less sensitive (40% vs. 24%). Our method also generalizes to reward-hacking models. A model organism trained to reward-hack (gpt-oss-120b) is more than twice as sensitive to grader preferences as the unmodified model, with the mean behavioral shift in favor of the grader rising from 33% to 86%. These results indicate that RL can increase reward-seeking over the course of training, producing models that may act against their developers' intentions when they believe that doing so leads to higher reward.

</details>


### [101] [Mi-Memory: A Lifecycle Memory Framework for Personal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18975)
*Xule Liu,Hanlin Teng,Chao Li,Yanan Ni,Shuo Lu,Audrey Wang,Yijun Liu,Yunfei Wang,Xiaofeng Li,Xian Yi,Yuanfa Li,Kang Zhao,Jian Liang,Yuxuan Chen,Jinyuan Chen,Heng Qu,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mi-Memory是一个面向个人AI的生命周期记忆框架，包含结构、扩展、演进和部署四个角色，通过审计契约和四类工件家族实现可审计、证据门控、部署感知的记忆管理。在受控引用评估中，MemStack在LoCoMo、PersonaMem-V2和LongMemEval上分别达到93.59%、57.24%和87.47%。项目主页：https://darwin-agent.github.io/Mi-Memory/


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI正从纯聊天交互向跨越手机、汽车、家居、可穿戴设备、摄像头和工具的连续服务演进。在此场景下，记忆不能仅仅是先前对话的缓存，而应成为连续性与治理的基础设施：保留持久用户状态、基于多模态和设备证据回答问题、支持纠正与遗忘、约束策略演化、并在延迟/成本/隐私/边云约束下可部署。

Method: 提出Mi-Memory框架，围绕四个角色组织：Structure（通过MemStack实现记忆结构）、Expansion（通过MemSense/MemFuse实现多模态证据扩展）、Evolution（通过D²ACCI/E²MEND实现策略演进）、Deployment（通过LiteMem实现轻量部署）。共享审计契约通过四类工件家族连接各角色：类型化证据载荷（保留来源和出处）、诊断追踪（定位管线中证据丢失）、策略工件（使记忆策略变化显式化）、门控/回滚记录（约束已接受的演化）。

Result: 在受控引用结构评估中，MemStack在LoCoMo上达93.59%，PersonaMem-V2上达57.24%，LongMemEval上达87.47%。其他模块报告了模块级、初步/内部、迁移可行性或仅设计层面的证据，且明确标注了边界。

Conclusion: Mi-Memory是迈向可审计、证据门控、部署感知的个人AI记忆系统的一步。

Abstract: Personal AI is moving beyond chat-only interaction toward continuous services that span phones, cars, homes, wearables, cameras, and tools. In this setting, memory cannot remain a cache of prior conversations. It should serve as a continuity and governance substrate: preserving durable user state, grounding answers in multimodal and device evidence, supporting correction and forgetting, bounding policy evolution, and remaining deployable under latency, cost, privacy, and edge-cloud constraints. This technical report presents Mi-Memory, a lifecycle memory framework for Personal AI organized around four roles: Structure, Expansion, Evolution, and Deployment. A shared audit contract links these roles through four recurring artifact families: typed evidence payloads preserve source identity and provenance, diagnostic traces localize evidence loss across the serving pipeline, strategy artifacts make memory-policy changes explicit, and gate/rollback records bound accepted evolution. MiMemory instantiates the roles through MemStack, MemSense/MemFuse, D$^{2}$ACCI/E$^{2}$MEND, and LiteMem. In controlled-reference Structure evaluations, MemStack reaches 93.59%, 57.24%, and 87.47% on LoCoMo, PersonaMem-V2, and LongMemEval, respectively; other tracks report module-level, preliminary/internal, transfer-feasibility, or design-only evidence with explicit boundaries. MiMemory is a step toward auditable, evidence-gated, and deployment-aware memory systems for Personal AI. Project homepage: https://darwin-agent.github.io/Mi-Memory/ .

</details>


### [102] [Fishing Out Free Riders: Shapley-Based Reward Attribution for Parallel Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18979)
*Wentao Zhang,Haoyu Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Yuxuan Cheng,Yuhan Pan,Miao Li,Zhipeng Qiao,Tao Feng,Zhen Tao,Dengji Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Parallel Shapley框架，利用Shapley值对多路径推理中各路径的边际贡献进行细粒度归因，通过生成式奖励模型和蒙特卡洛采样实现路径级奖励分配，解决传统并行推理中奖励信号模糊和训练不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并行推理方法难以区分各推理路径的贡献，许多路径存在冗余、误导甚至有害的情况，但基于结果级奖励将所有路径视为等同，导致学习信号模糊、训练不稳定。

Method: 将每条推理路径视为合作博弈中的玩家，利用Shapley值量化路径的边际贡献；采用生成式奖励模型评估路径效用，并结合蒙特卡洛采样高效近似计算Shapley值，实现路径级别的奖励分配。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Parallel Shapley优于现有基线方法，并且训练更稳定、更具可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效"揪出搭便车者"，按比例分配奖励，从而提升LLM多路径推理的效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at multi-step reasoning, yet current parallel reasoning approaches often fail to distinguish the contributions of individual reasoning paths. Many paths may be redundant, misleading, or even detrimental, but outcome-level rewards assign uniform reward, leading to ambiguous learning signals and unstable training. We propose Parallel Shapley, a reinforcement learning framework that attributes fine-grained, path-level contributions in multi-path reasoning. Treating each path as a player in a cooperative game, we leverage Shapley values to quantify marginal contributions, using a generative reward model to evaluate path utilities and Monte Carlo sampling for efficient approximation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Parallel Shapley outperforms existing baselines while providing more stable and interpretable training. Our framework effectively "fishes out the free riders," assigning reward proportionally and improving multi-path reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [103] [Athena-Brain Technical Report: An Efficient Robot Brain for General Intelligence and Embodied Interactio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18985)
*Jialian Li,Junhong Liu,Yuchen Cao,Weiran Guo,Jiaming Song,Xutao Wang,Yi Zhao,Jiangpin Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Athena-Brain-8B，一个8B参数的大语言模型，通过多阶段后训练（通用SFT、通用RL、具身专家训练、模型融合）在保持通用智能的同时获得高效具身交互能力，在通用和具身基准上均表现优异，且生成更简洁的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时满足具身智能体对「通用大语言模型智能」和「专用具身能力」的双重要求，缺乏能在设备端紧凑部署且兼具两者优势的模型。

Method: 采用多阶段后训练流程：通用监督微调（SFT）→ 通用强化学习（RL）→ 具身专家训练 → 模型融合（Model Merge），在Qwen3-8B思维模型基础上进行优化。

Result: 在通用语言和推理基准上与Qwen3-8B性能相当但响应更短；在领域内具身基准上优于同规模模型，并零样本超越多个更大规模的先进模型。

Conclusion: 紧凑语言模型可以有效地将通用智能与具身能力进行整合，为设备端具身智能提供可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in language understanding, reasoning, and world knowledge. As embodied agents become increasingly capable, there is a growing demand for compact models that can serve as an on-device brain, preserving the broad general intelligence of LLMs while enabling effective high-level interaction with embodied environments. Existing approaches, however, often prioritize either general-purpose intelligence or specialized embodied capabilities, making it challenging to satisfy both requirements within a single model. We present \textbf{Athena-Brain-8B}, an 8B LLM designed to serve as an on-device brain for embodied intelligence for embodied intelligence. Through a multi-stage post-training pipeline consisting of General Supervised Fine-Tuning, General Reinforcement Learning, Embodied Expert training, and Model Merge, Athena-Brain-8B maintains strong general capabilities while acquiring strong high-level embodied interaction capabilities and generating concise responses for efficient embodied interaction. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of Athena across both general and embodied evaluations. Compared with the corresponding Qwen3-8B thinking model, Athena-Brain-8B achieves comparable performance on general language and reasoning benchmarks while generating substantially shorter responses. On in-domain embodied benchmarks, Athena-Brain-8B consistently outperforms models of similar scale and surpasses several substantially larger frontier models evaluated zero-shot, demonstrating that compact language models can effectively integrate strong general intelligence with embodied capabilities.

</details>


### [104] [Vector-Bench: Can Models Surgically Edit SVG Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19056)
*Yug Aditi Gupta,Prannay Hebbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Vector-Bench，一个包含40个SVG修复任务的基准测试，用于评估指令驱动的矢量编辑能力。最强的模型端点仅达到15.0%的完整规范成功率，表明修复进展与规范忠实编辑之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅以光栅图像判断输出，容易忽略"保持未请求部分不变"这一能力。作者旨在提供一个紧凑但具有挑战性的基准，严格评估模型在矢量编辑中同时执行请求变更和保持其余部分不变的能力。

Method: 构建40个SVG修复任务，每个任务包含损坏的SVG程序、作者编写的视觉指令、隐藏目标程序、平均5.05个带注释的修复和平均60.55个受保护对象。定义确定性二元规范奖励，使用属性感知感知容差评估请求修复，同时确保未请求的渲染或应用相关结构语义不变且输出为有效SVG。评估34个模型端点共1360个请求。

Result: 最强端点仅达到15.0%的完整规范成功率，尽管平均修复进展为43.7%。有效输出的意外变更率（UCR）等指标揭示了部分成功的原因。

Conclusion: 明显的修复进展与规范忠实编辑之间存在实质性差异，表明现有模型在精确指令遵循和保持未修改区域完整性方面仍有显著改进空间。所有提示、输出、评分代码、成本和每任务报告均已发布。

Abstract: Instruction-based vector editing requires two capabilities: making a requested change and leaving everything else alone. The second is easy to miss when an output is judged only as a raster image. We introduce Vector-Bench, a compact, difficult benchmark of 40 SVG repair tasks. Each task pairs a corrupted SVG program with an author-written visual instruction, a hidden target program, 5.05 annotated repairs on average, and an average of 60.55 protected objects. Instructions describe visible defects without exposing element identifiers, coordinates, color codes, or path data. We define a deterministic binary specification reward: requested repairs use attribute-aware perceptual tolerances, while unrequested rendering- or application-relevant structure must remain semantically unchanged and the result must be a valid SVG. Canonical target equality and stricter source fidelity are retained as diagnostics. Validity-gated repair progress, a near-complete tier, and valid-output Unintended Change Rate (UCR) explain partial outcomes. We evaluate 34 model endpoints (25 listed as open-weight, 5 inexpensive controls, and 4 frontier closed endpoints) over 1360 requests. The strongest endpoint reaches only 15.0% full specification success, despite 43.7% mean repair progress, showing that apparent repair progress and specification-faithful editing remain substantially different. All prompts, outputs, scoring code, costs, and per-task reports are released.

</details>


### [105] [Supra Cognitive Modes: A Routed Architecture for Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19096)
*Joshua Tobkin,David Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM)架构，将查询路由至不同检索与合成模式，在共享基座上处理智能体记忆任务，在三个基准上取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆工作负载混合了直接事实查找、关系链与当前状态推理以及长历史综合，现有方案缺乏统一架构来高效处理这些异质查询模式。

Method: SCM架构包含冻结语义分类器与运行时门控机制，将查询路由至融合词汇与密集查找、图或多跳迭代推理、分层长形式综合三类模式；基座结合多粒度嵌入、抽取三元组、事实版本元数据及异步增强。

Result: LoCoMo事实类84.87%、对抗性弃权68.61%；MAB两轮重复61.49%；LongMemEval 86.00%。

Conclusion: SCM架构在多个智能体记忆基准上取得强性能，但因果路由效应、效率提升及统计显著性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Agent-memory workloads mix direct factual lookup, relation-chain and current-state reasoning, and broad synthesis over long histories. We describe Supra Cognitive Modes (SCM), an architecture that maps explicit or automatically selected per-query modes to retrieval and synthesis payloads over one shared ingest substrate. A frozen semantic classifier and runtime gates dispatch queries among fused lexical and dense lookup, graph or iterative multi-hop handling, and stratified long-form synthesis. The substrate combines multi-granularity embeddings, extracted triples, fact-version metadata, and optional asynchronous enrichments. We characterize the deployed configuration on three benchmarks: Long-term Conversational Memory (LoCoMo; n = 1,986), MemoryAgentBench (MAB; n = 3,671), and LongMemEval (n = 500). The reference run records 84.87% on LoCoMo factoid categories and 68.61% on adversarial abstention, 61.49% on MAB across two repetitions, and 86.00% on LongMemEval. A repository-backed reproduction produces similar aggregate scores and supports task- and mode-conditioned failure analysis. Raw baseline outputs, aligned end-to-end timing for LoCoMo and LongMemEval, and complete token ledgers are unavailable; stored rows also omit some final runtime decisions. The results characterize one implemented routed configuration and its diagnostic failure patterns, while source inspection verifies the per-query control interface and shared-substrate design. Causal routing effects, efficiency gains, and statistical significance remain outside the available evidence.

</details>


### [106] [Quality Action Assurance: Multimodal Verification of Examiner Claims in VR OSCEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19063)
*Harry Rogers,Sally Shiels,Ashley Tomlinson,James Thomas,James Aylward,Nathan Gauge,Helen Higham,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QAA多模态框架，通过约束时间对齐与LLM结合，在VR儿科OSCE中验证考官声称，将事实正确率从39.2%提升至79.2%，实现更公平的临床能力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSCE评分受考官主观性、疲劳和认知偏差影响，传统评分者间信度统计无法解释错误来源，缺乏对考官推理的分析和事实核查机制。

Method: QAA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 约束时间动作对齐模型，实现动作定位与行为者来源归因；(2) 大语言模型，用于提取考官声称并与视频、VR日志、演员数据构建的真实事件序列进行比对验证。

Result: 5折交叉验证中，QAA达到99.2%±0.7%的Actor F1和93.4%±1.9%的W@16时间对齐精度；检测考官错误时精确率70.0%，召回率76.7%，事实正确率从39.2%提升至79.2%。

Conclusion: QAA通过多模态事实核查有效检测和纠正考官评分错误，为OSCE评估提供了更客观、更公平的验证手段。

Abstract: Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs) are the gold standard for assessing clinical competence, yet scoring remains vulnerable to examiner subjectivity, fatigue, and cognitive bias. Standard examiner validation via inter-rater statistics lacks explanatory power regarding the source of errors, as it neither analyzes examiner reasoning nor verifies examiner claims against actual events. Thus, we introduce Quality Action Assurance (QAA), a multimodal framework that verifies examiner claims in Virtual Reality (VR) pediatric OSCEs by comparing actions claimed by examiners against the true sequence of events, constructed from video, VR logs, and actor data. QAA combines a constrained temporal action alignment model, which performs action localization and actor source attribution, with a large language model that extracts examiner claims and checks them against the record. Across a 5-fold cross-validation, QAA achieves 99.2% $\pm$ 0.7% Actor F1 and 93.4% $\pm$ 1.9% W@16 for temporal alignment. Overall, QAA detects examiner errors with 70.0% precision and 76.7% recall, improving factual correctness from 39.2% to 79.2%, enabling fairer OSCE assessment.

</details>


### [107] [Agents in the Wild: Where Research Meets Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19336)
*Grace Hui Yang,Pranav N. Venkit,Hooman Sedghamiz,Enrico Santus,Victor Dibia,Ioana Baldini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于大语言模型的智能体系统在从研究原型向生产级部署过渡过程中面临的鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性挑战，通过制药发现和金融系统等案例，总结了成功的设计模式及失败缓解策略（验证流水线、回退机制、人在回路监督），并为行业部署提供了评估清单和模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学术界关注基准测试和算法创新，但实际部署暴露了智能体系统在鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性方面的新挑战，亟需系统化的设计模式和缓解策略指导。

Method: 通过汇集研究人员和从业者，结合制药发现和金融系统的应用案例，分析推理规划、多智能体协调和评估方面的进展，总结常见设计模式及实践缓解策略。

Result: 成功的设计模式包括验证流水线、回退机制和人在回路监督等；提供了评估检查清单和跨行业部署模板。

Conclusion: 智能体系统从研究到生产的过渡需要关注鲁棒性、安全性和可靠性，本文提供的设计模式和缓解策略可指导行业的安全可靠部署。

Abstract: Agentic systems large language model (LLM) based architectures capable of reasoning, planning, acting, and coordinating with tools and other agents are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to production scale deployments across domains such as software engineering, scientific discovery, and finance. While academic work has emphasized benchmarks and algorithmic innovation, deployment raises new challenges around robustness, safety, and reliability. This tutorial brings together researchers and practitioners to explore advances in reasoning and planning, multi agent coordination, and evaluation, highlighting open challenges arising from deployment experience. Through applied case studies in pharmaceutical discovery and financial systems, we analyze common design patterns that make agentic systems successful, and discuss practical mitigation strategies for failure modes, such as verification pipelines, fallback mechanisms, and human in the loop supervision. Attendees will gain a comprehensive view of the field along with concrete design patterns, evaluation checklists, and templates for safe and reliable deployment across industries.

</details>


### [108] [On the Effectiveness of Pretraining for Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19072)
*David Aguado,Daniel Fuertes,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对路由问题（如TSP）的自监督预训练框架，通过几何增广（旋转和反射）的图对比学习，学习不变结构表示和全局相对距离分布，在TSP1000上实现6.57%的路径长度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经求解器在扩展到高维路由问题时性能下降，缺乏有效的预训练策略来学习几何不变性表示。

Method: 采用自监督图对比学习框架，使用旋转和轴向反射作为几何增广策略，迫使模型学习不变的结构表示和全局相对距离分布。

Result: 预训练策略在多个问题规模上均优于非预训练模型，混合策略（旋转+反射）在TSP1000上路径长度提升6.57%。

Conclusion: 几何预训练是有效扩展神经求解器到高维实例的重要归纳偏置。

Abstract: This paper introduces a self-supervised pretraining framework for graph combinatorial optimization specifically designed to address the nature of routing problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem. By utilizing graph contrastive learning with geometric augmentations (specifically, rotations and axial reflections) the model is forced to learn invariant structural representations and global relative distance distributions. Results demonstrate that this pretraining strategy outperforms non-pretrained models across various problem scales. Notably, the hybrid strategy (combining rotation and reflection) achieved a 6.57% improvement in tour length for TSP1000, proving that geometric pretraining is an important inductive bias for effectively scaling neural solvers to high-dimensional instances.

</details>


### [109] [OpenRTAG: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robust Text-Attributed Graph Learning under Data Quality Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19108)
*Yuze Dai,Zhihan Zhang,Yan Zhao,Ruoyu Wu,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Qiangqiang Dai,Hongchao Qin,Ronghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenRTAG基准，系统研究文本属性图在文本、结构和标签三个维度上的稀疏、噪声和不平衡等质量问题，构建3×3分类体系，在9个数据集上评估多种模型（GNN、LLM-GNN、GFM）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的文本属性图常存在文本、结构和标签层面的质量缺陷（稀疏、噪声、不平衡），但现有研究碎片化，缺乏统一的鲁棒性评估框架。

Method: 构建3×3（文本/结构/标签 × 稀疏/噪声/不平衡）统一分类体系，在9个TAG数据集和3个下游任务上系统评估场景有效性和模型敏感性，对比传统GNN、LLM-GNN和图基础模型，并研究复合退化场景。

Result: 建立了标准化鲁棒性测试平台，系统揭示了不同退化场景下各类模型的表现差异和鲁棒性特性。

Conclusion: OpenRTAG填补了TAG学习鲁棒性缺乏统一基准的空白，为低质量设置下的鲁棒TAG学习提供了标准化测试环境。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) are an important graph data form that combine relational structure with rich node text. However, real-world TAGs are often imperfect, with quality issues arising from text, structure, and labels, and typically manifesting as sparsity, noise, and imbalance. These dimensions define nine representative degradation scenarios that can substantially affect TAG learning. Although prior studies have explored specific mitigation strategies, existing evidence remains fragmented across degradation types, datasets, tasks, and model families, leaving TAG robustness insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we present OpenRTAG, a robustness benchmark for text-attributed graph learning. OpenRTAG organizes TAG quality issues into a unified 3 * 3 taxonomy and supports standardized evaluation across nine TAG datasets and three downstream tasks. It systematically evaluates scenario validity and model sensitivity, compares traditional GNNs, LLM-GNNs, and a representative GFM, investigates the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of scenario-matched baselines, and further examines model behavior under composite degradation scenarios. OpenRTAG provides a standardized testbed for understanding robustness in TAG learning under realistic low-quality settings.

</details>


### [110] [Comparative Study of Multi-Agent Actor-Critic Algorithms in Parameterized Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19117)
*Ubayd Ali Bapoo,Clement N Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于共享经验的多智能体参数化动作强化学习方法（MAGAC、MASAC、MATQC），通过独立Actor-Critic智能体共享经验池，在Platform-v0和Goal-v0基准上评估。结果表明多智能体框架显著提升GAC性能，但MASAC和MATQC增益有限；超过5个智能体后性能提升有限且计算成本剧增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动作强化学习在需要离散动作选择和连续参数化的环境中表现出色，但现有方法（GAC、SAC、TQC）局限于单智能体场景，缺乏向多智能体环境的扩展研究。

Method: 提出共享经验的多智能体扩展框架，每个智能体维护独立的策略网络和价值网络，但共享一个经验回放缓冲池，而非采用CTDE范式。在3、5、10智能体配置下与单智能体基线对比，使用平均评估回报和训练时间作为指标，并采用单因素方差分析和Tukey HSD事后检验评估统计显著性。

Result: 多智能体框架显著提升GAC性能；MASAC和MATQC相比单智能体版本提升有限；智能体数量超过5个后性能增益递减但计算成本显著增加，尤其对MAGAC而言。

Conclusion: 揭示了共享经验多智能体Actor-Critic方法在参数化动作强化学习中的学习性能与计算效率之间的权衡，为方法的可扩展性提供了洞见。

Abstract: Parameterized action reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in environments requiring both discrete action selection and continuous parameterization. Prior work established the effectiveness of single-agent actor-critic algorithms - Greedy Actor-Critic (GAC), Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), and Truncated Quantile Critics (TQC) - on benchmark parameterized action tasks, but their extension to multi-agent settings remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a comparative study of shared-experience multi-agent extensions of these algorithms: Multi-Agent Greedy Actor-Critic (MAGAC), Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC), and Multi-Agent Truncated Quantile Critics (MATQC). Rather than following the centralized training, decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm, the proposed framework uses multiple independent actor-critic agents that share a replay buffer while maintaining separate policy and value networks. We evaluate the algorithms on the Platform-v0 and Goal-v0 benchmarks against their single-agent counterparts, using three-, five-, and ten-agent configurations to assess scalability. Performance is measured by average evaluation return and training time across ten independent runs, with one-way ANOVA and Tukey HSD post-hoc tests used to assess statistical significance. Results show that the multi-agent framework consistently improves Greedy Actor-Critic performance, while MASAC and MATQC show comparatively modest gains over their single-agent versions. Increasing the number of agents beyond five yields limited additional performance while substantially raising computational cost, particularly for MAGAC. These results highlight a trade-off between learning performance and computational efficiency, offering insight into the scalability of shared-experience multi-agent actor-critic methods for parameterized action reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [111] [Sequential Learner Modeling Using Multi-Relational Graph Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19253)
*Rawaa Alatrash,Mohamed Amine Chatti,Hong Yang,Yumeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-ConceptGCN，一种结合多关系图卷积网络、个人知识图谱和SBERT的无监督序列学习用户建模方法，在线用户研究（n=31）证明其在教育推荐系统中在准确性、有用性、多样性和满意度方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN用户建模方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 将图中不同关系类型视为同质的，限制了语义捕获能力；(2) 忽略了用户交互序列信息。多关系GNN虽已用于推荐但未用于用户建模。

Method: MR-ConceptGCN融合个人知识图谱(PKG)、多关系图卷积网络(MR-GCN)和预训练语言模型SBERT，获取PKG项目的增强关系和语义感知表示；利用学习者在CourseMapper中互动时未理解的知识概念嵌入，构建结合长期和短期交互的序列学习者模型。

Result: 在线用户研究（n=31）显示，MR-ConceptGCN在教育推荐系统中在准确性、有用性、多样性和满意度等多个用户中心指标上表现优异。

Conclusion: 多关系GCN结合序列交互信息可显著提升基于概念的用户建模效果，为教育推荐系统提供更精准、多样且令人满意的个性化服务。

Abstract: User modeling is a critical task in a variety of personalized systems. Recognizing their effectiveness in learning from graph-structured data, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), particularly Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), are increasingly employed for user modeling. However, existing approaches typically treat different relation types in a graph as homogeneous, limiting their ability to capture richer semantics and construct more informative user models. While multi-relational GNNs (MR-GNNs) have been adopted for representation learning and recommendation, their application for user modeling remains unexplored. Moreover, existing GNN-based user modeling approaches ignore the user interaction sequence. To address these research gaps, in this work we propose MR-ConceptGCN, a novel fully unsupervised approach focused on concept-based sequential learner modeling using multi-relational GCNs (MR-GCNs). MR-ConceptGCN effecively combines Personal Knowledge Graphs (PKGs), MR-GCNs, and the pre-trained language model SBERT to obtain enhanced relation- and semantic-aware representations of the PKG items. The enriched embeddings of the knowledge concepts that a learner did not understand when interacting with learning materials in CourseMapper are then used to construct a sequential learner model that combines long-term and short-term learner interactions. We report the results of an online user study (n = 31), demonstrating the benefits of MR-ConceptGCN in terms of several important user-centric aspects including accuracy, usefulness, diversity, and satisfaction with an educational recommender system.

</details>


### [112] [BioSecBench-Surveillance: A Verifiable Benchmark for AI Agents in Pathogen Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19262)
*Harmon Bhasin,Kevin Flyangolts,Dianzhuo Wang,Evan Seeyave,Arjun Banerjee,Amanda Darling,Joshua Stallings,David Stern,Shawn Higdon,Claire Duvallet,Bryan Tegomoh,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioSecBench-Surveillance基准测试，包含100个评估任务，用于测试AI代理能否从原始测序数据和监测背景中推断出正确的分析流程。最强配置（Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex）仅通过约50%的测试，说明AI代理在基因组监测分析中仍存在显著不足，错误主要源于对参考数据库、阈值、过滤器和归一化等参数的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着病原体基因组监测规模的扩大，数据分析已成为新的瓶颈，亟需一个标准化的基准来评估AI代理是否能够可靠地执行基因组监测分析任务。

Method: 构建了BioSecBench-Surveillance基准，包含100个可验证的评估任务，覆盖7个类别（从分类学分类到基因工程检测），涉及多种样本类型和测序技术。每个评估仅提供人类分析师可获取的数据和背景信息，然后对AI代理的结构化答案进行确定性评分。

Result: 在来自16个模型-工具组合的3,962次可评分尝试中，最强配置仅通过约50%。Opus 4.8 with PI和GPT-5.5 with Codex并列最高（50.2%），其次是Opus 4.7 with PI（49.6%）和Sonnet 4.6 with PI（48.6%）。即使代理调用了正确的工作流，错误也主要源于对参考数据库、阈值、过滤器和归一化等参数的选择。

Conclusion: BioSecBench-Surveillance为衡量AI代理在下一次疫情爆发时是否可被信任执行基因组监测提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: As pathogen genomic surveillance scales, the bottleneck is shifting from data generation to analysis. We present BioSecBench-Surveillance, a verifiable benchmark of 100 evaluations testing whether AI agents can infer the right analysis pipeline from raw sequencing data and surveillance context. Each evaluation gives an agent only the data and context a human analyst would have, then grades its structured answer deterministically. The tasks span seven categories, from taxonomic classification to genetic-engineering detection, across diverse sample types and sequencing technologies. Across 3,962 gradable attempts from sixteen model-harness pairs, the strongest configuration cleared only about half. Opus 4.8 with PI led at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.1 to 60.3 percent across 83 evaluations, tied with GPT-5.5 with Codex at 50.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.8 to 59.6 percent, followed by Opus 4.7 with PI at 49.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 40.0 to 59.2 percent, and Sonnet 4.6 with PI at 48.6 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 38.9 to 58.3 percent. Even when agents invoked the correct workflows, their mistakes came from the choices around them, such as which references, thresholds, filters, and normalization to apply. BioSecBench-Surveillance provides a standard for measuring whether agents can be trusted to perform genomic surveillance when the next outbreak arrives.

</details>


### [113] [Graph-Based Agentic AI with LangGraph: Workflow Pathways for Long-Running Stateful Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19297)
*Daniel Pearson,Sidney Shapiro,Emiliano Sebastian Gonzalez Venegas,Sanad Al-Khatib,Aurora Pinzón Arzola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇实用指南，展示了如何使用LangGraph框架为长时间运行、有状态、多步骤的生成式AI业务流程构建基于图的工作流路径，包含三个可执行方案：带修复循环的SQL分析、带证据门控的检索增强生成、以及带中断与检查点恢复的人工参与策略审核。文章通过工作流复杂度匹配来定位LangGraph的适用场景，而非将其视为通用默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实践者提供关于何时以及如何使用LangGraph进行有状态、多步骤生成式AI业务流程的具体指导，澄清LangGraph并非通用默认框架，而是针对复杂工作流场景的专业工具。

Method: 通过三个可执行方案（SQL分析修复循环、带证据门控的RAG、人工在环策略审核）展示LangGraph的核心机制：类型化状态、条件路由、确定性工具、重试、中断、检查点和追踪，并与ReAct模式、SDK循环、schema优先工具和DSPy等替代方案进行场景对比。

Result: 提出了LangGraph的适用边界：适合需要显式路由、暂停和审计轨迹的复杂工作流；简单工具调用场景应使用ReAct或SDK循环，结构化提取验证应使用schema优先工具，提示优化应使用DSPy。

Conclusion: LangGraph的价值在于将路由、暂停和审计轨迹转化为显式的产品行为而非隐藏的提示逻辑，需要在工作流复杂度达到一定阈值时才值得引入额外的结构成本。

Abstract: This paper is a practitioner guide to graph-based workflow pathways for long-running, stateful, multi-step generative AI systems in business processes. Rather than treating LangGraph, a low-level orchestration framework for stateful agents, as a model-quality benchmark target, we present three executable recipes -- SQL analytics with repair loops, agentic retrieval-augmented generation with evidence gating, and human-in-the-loop policy review with interrupt and checkpoint recovery -- to show how typed state, conditional routing, deterministic tools, retries, interrupts, checkpoints, and traces fit together. LangGraph is positioned by workflow-complexity fit, not as a universal default: simpler ReAct-style or plain SDK loops may be better for basic tool use, schema-first tools for structured extraction and validation, and DSPy when prompt or program optimization is the main goal. Each recipe explains when LangGraph is worth the extra structure and which implementation patterns make routes, pauses, and audit trails explicit product behavior rather than hidden prompt logic.

</details>


### [114] [LLM Detection as an Intervention: Downstream Impact under Strategic User Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19300)
*Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Jon Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建一个战略博弈模型，揭示了不完美的LLM检测器如何通过改变用户使用LLM的激励机制，导致LLM使用量意外增加、输出质量下降等反直觉后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM检测工具被广泛采用，人们对其干预效果的理解尚不充分。现有研究主要关注检测器的准确性本身，而忽视了它如何通过改变用户行为来影响LLM使用量和输出质量等下游指标。

Method: 构建了一个风格化博弈模型，刻画用户在选择使用LLM程度以及如何后处理生成内容以降低检测属性时的策略性行为。并通过arXiv摘要中的词频数据进行了实证复现。

Result: 检测器会导致被检测属性呈现清晰的"先升后降"模式；反直觉地，LLM检测可能使用户增加LLM使用量；即使在降低被检测属性有助于提升输出质量的情况下，引入检测器仍可能导致用户产出更低质量的内容。

Conclusion: LLM检测作为一种干预手段，其作用于下游指标时存在失效模式，可能扭曲LLM使用行为和输出质量，警示在部署检测器时需要谨慎考虑其激励效应。

Abstract: As LLM adoption becomes more widespread, there is a growing interest in detecting LLM-generated content, for example through LLM detection tools and through heuristics based on language patterns. Detectors operate as an intervention that steers not only the detected attribute itself, but also downstream metrics such as LLM usage and output quality. In this work, we demonstrate how imperfect LLM detectors lead to counterintuitive impacts on these downstream metrics, by distorting how users are incentivized to use LLMs in their workflow. We develop a stylized model which captures how users strategically choose how much to use the LLM and how to post-process content to reduce the detected attribute. Using this model, we show that LLM detection can counterintuitively lead humans to increase their LLM usage. Moreover, even when reducing the detected attribute improves output quality, we find that introducing an LLM detector can lead users to produce lower quality outputs. In contrast, we show that detectors result in a clean "rise-then-fall" pattern for the detected attribute, which we empirically reproduce for word frequencies on arXiv abstracts. Altogether, our work illustrates how LLM detection can distort LLM usage and output quality, uncovering failure modes when LLM detectors operate as an intervention on these downstream metrics.

</details>


### [115] [Associative Emotional Learning in Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19327)
*Seowung Leem,Andreas Keil,Mingzhou Ding,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度神经网络模型，用于模拟视觉效价加工的联想情绪学习，该模型在巴甫洛夫条件学习范式下成功复现了人类联想学习中的关联形成、泛化以及神经表征对齐等行为与神经特征，并通过与人类实验数据的比较验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算模型（如Rescorla-Wagner模型）在解释联想情绪学习方面存在局限性，尤其是应用于神经数据时。深度神经网络的兴起为建模联想情绪学习提供了新途径，本文旨在探索这一方向。

Method: 构建了一个深度神经网络模型，包含视觉模块（编码复杂自然场景）和情绪识别模块（识别效价维度）。在该模型上测试了一种新的巴甫洛夫条件学习范式。

Result: 模型通过学习复现了人类联想学习研究的多个现象，包括关联形成和泛化；条件刺激与非条件刺激的神经表征在单单元和神经群体水平上趋于对齐；与人类实验数据的比较进一步验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络模型结合适当的学习算法，可用于建模联想情绪/效价学习的行为和神经印记。

Abstract: Associative emotional learning enables organisms to adaptively link pleasant or unpleasant outcomes to the presence of predictive stimuli. Whereas computational models such as the Rescorla-Wagner model have shed light on this important function, the limitations of these models are also known, especially when they are applied to neural data. The advent of deep neural networks has opened another avenue for modeling associative emotional learning. In this work we proposed a deep neural network model of visual valence processing, consisting of a visual module that encodes complex natural scenes and a module that recognizes their emotional significance in terms of valence, a key dimension of emotion, and tested a novel Pavlovian learning paradigm on the model. The results showed that with learning, the model reproduced several observations from human associative learning studies, including association formation and generalization, and that the neural representations of the conditioned and the unconditioned stimuli became increasingly aligned both at the single unit and at the neural population level. Comparison between the model and human experimental data provided further validation of our approach. This study thus suggests that deep neural network models, when combined with appropriate learning algorithms, can be used to model behavioral and neural signatures of associative emotion/valence learning.

</details>


### [116] [CodeRescue: Budget-Calibrated Recovery Routing for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19338)
*Qijia He,Jiayi Cheng,Chenqian Le,Rui Wang,Xunmei Liu,Yixian Chen,Jie Mei,Zhihao Wang,Xupeng Chen,Yuhuan Chen,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行反馈的预算感知路由框架，通过监督训练恢复路由器和保形风险控制层，在代码代理中实现低成本恢复与高成本升级之间的最优决策，在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4设置下以35%的恢复成本达到甚至超越始终升级的求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成本感知系统通常将失败视为级联决策（先用廉价模型，失败后升级到昂贵模型），但在可执行环境中，执行反馈本身可能使廉价模型的进一步尝试（恢复）有价值。因此需要回答：何时应花费更多廉价计算资源进行恢复，何时应直接升级。

Method: 1) 将失败后决策形式化为异构动作上的恢复路由问题；2) 从执行轨迹中监督训练一个路由器；3) 引入保形风险控制（Conformal Risk Control, CRC）层，在不重新训练的情况下选择部署时的成本惩罚，并在可交换性假设下提供边际期望成本控制。

Result: 在五个编码基准的失败样本上，廉价恢复和升级展现出互补的成功模式。校准前沿优于固定动作、仅提示路由器和二元级联基线。在GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4主设置中，一个CRC校准前沿点以35%的平均恢复成本达到甚至超越始终升级的求解率。

Conclusion: 执行反馈使廉价恢复具有价值，通过监督学习与保形风险控制相结合，可以在不同预算下实现灵活的路由决策，在显著降低成本的同时保持甚至提升求解性能。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly operate in executable environments where a failed attempt produces actionable feedback rather than merely an incorrect answer. Existing cost-aware systems typically treat such failures as cascade decisions: try a cheap model first, then escalate hard cases to a stronger and more expensive model. In coding, however, execution feedback can also make further cheap-model recovery worthwhile, raising a budgeted deployment question: when should an agent spend more cheap compute, and when should it escalate? We formulate this post-failure decision as recovery routing over heterogeneous actions and train a supervised router from execution rollouts. To make the same router usable under changing budgets, we add a Conformal Risk Control (CRC) layer that selects a deployment-time cost penalty without retraining and provides marginal expected-cost control under exchangeability. Across held-out failures from five coding benchmarks, cheap recovery and escalation exhibit complementary success patterns. The calibrated frontier improves over fixed actions, prompt-only routers, and a binary cascade baseline; in the main GPT-5.4-nano/GPT-5.4 setting, one CRC-calibrated frontier point exceeds always-escalate solve rate while using 35% of its mean recovery cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Qijia-He/agent-budget-control.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Shared Vulnerabilities in Robustness-Optimized Defenses: One Breach Exposes the Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18339)
*Hanrui Wang,Ruihao Zheng,Shuo Wang,Isao Echizen,Xingbo Dong,Zhe Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现对抗鲁棒性优化（如对抗训练和对抗净化）会产生跨防御的共享脆弱性：一旦击破一种代表性鲁棒优化防御，整个防御家族可能被暴露。作者引入更严格的纯迁移协议和简单自适应攻击PGDTransfer来验证这一风险，并在对抗训练分类器、净化防御和LVLMs中均发现自然迁移性。特别地，PGDTransfer在净化防御上平均迁移攻击成功率高达80.4%，表明净化防御可能不再可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别对抗鲁棒性优化方法（对抗训练、对抗净化等）可能引入的共享脆弱性安全风险：这些方法虽然提升了单个模型的鲁棒性，但可能在整个防御家族中创造共有的、可被迁移攻击利用的脆弱点。

Method: (1) 提出更严格的纯迁移协议（transfer-only protocols），区分真正的迁移性与扰动退化及高级攻击算法增益；(2) 设计简单自适应攻击PGDTransfer，在受控条件下测试鲁棒优化防御是否共享纯迁移脆弱性；(3) 引入对抗敏感性图（AdvSMs），用于可视化和量化超越可微分类器的共享对齐性，涵盖随机和非可微防御。

Result: 在对抗训练分类器、净化防御（过滤、压缩、扩散）和具有鲁棒视觉编码器的LVLMs中，均发现每个鲁棒家族内部存在自然迁移性。对净化防御，PGDTransfer在ε=4/255下平均迁移攻击成功率达80.4%，表明净化防御可能不再提供可靠保护。

Conclusion: 未来防御应将脆弱性多样性和纯迁移隔离作为安全目标，而不仅仅是优化个体鲁棒性。随着攻击进步，当前更强的鲁棒家族可能面临同样风险。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness optimization aims to preserve correct prediction under adversarial perturbations, and has produced substantial robustness gains through methods such as adversarial training and adversarial purification. However, we identify a new security risk: these gains can create shared vulnerabilities across defenses. Once one representative robustness-optimized defense is effectively breached, the broader family may become exposed. Studying this risk requires separating genuine transferability from distortion-induced degradation and from the algorithmic gains of sophisticated attacks. We therefore introduce stricter transfer-only protocols and a deliberately simple adaptive attack, PGDTransfer, to test whether robustness-optimized defenses share transfer-only vulnerability under controlled conditions. We further introduce Adversarial Sensitivity Maps (AdvSMs) to visualize and quantify shared alignment beyond differentiable classifiers, including stochastic and non-differentiable defenses. Across adversarially trained classifiers, purification-based defenses, and LVLMs with robust visual encoders, we identify natural transferability within each robustness family, i.e., transfer that arises even with simple PGD-style optimization rather than specialized transferable-attack design. The risk is already severe for purification: PGDTransfer reaches an average transfer attack success rate of $80.4\%$ across filtering-, compression-, and diffusion-based purifiers under $ε=4/255$, suggesting that purifier defenses may no longer provide reliable protection. As attacks improve, currently stronger robustness families may face the same risk. Future defenses should therefore treat vulnerability diversity and transfer-only isolation as security objectives, rather than optimizing only individual robustness.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum Cryptanalysis on IBM Quantum Hardware: Extending Even--Mansour Period Recovery from $N=4$ to $N=10$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18340)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Junghoon Shin,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在真实IBM量子硬件（Heron代）上实现了多个对称密码结构的量子攻击，包括使用Simon算法恢复Even-Mansour密码的密钥（安全参数N=10，此前最高为N=4）以及3轮Feistel结构的周期恢复，并系统 benchmark了五种量子攻击算法（Bernstein-Vazirani、Grover、Simon）在四种对称密码范式上的表现，上限为25量子比特的经典模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前真实量子硬件上对对称密码的密钥恢复攻击规模有限（最高N=4），且缺乏对多种量子攻击算法的系统性基准测试。本文旨在展示更大规模（N=10）的真实硬件量子攻击，并提供覆盖多种密码设计范式的可复现基准。

Method: 使用Simon算法在IBM真实量子硬件（ibm_kingston）上对Even-Mansour密码和3轮Feistel结构进行周期恢复；同时实现Bernstein-Vazirani、Grover、Simon等五种量子攻击算法，在四种对称密码范式上运行，利用错误缓解而非容错纠错，在经典模拟上限25量子比特内验证。

Result: 成功在真实硬件上恢复Even-Mansour密码密钥至N=10（此前最高N=4）；恢复3轮Feistel结构在块大小6和8上的周期；实现了21量子比特的块10实例模拟并提交硬件。五种量子攻击在25量子比特内完成经典模拟验证。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于在真实量子硬件上以创纪录的结构规模展示了对称密码的量子攻击，覆盖四种密码设计范式，提供了诚实、可复现的基准测试和公开工件。攻击限于Q2模型、简化或结构化构造，遵循生日界，未实现相对于经典碰撞搜索的量子优势，也未破解完整AES/RSA或16轮DES。

Abstract: We report genuine-un-compiled, textbook-faithful-quantum cryptanalysis of symmetric-cipher structures executed on real IBM quantum hardware (ibm\_kingston, Heron generation). Using Simon's algorithm we recover the hidden period of the Even-Mansour cipher up to security parameter N = 10 on real hardware, beyond the largest previously reported real-hardware key recovery of N = 4, and we cleanly recover the periods of a 3-round Feistel (DES-family) construction at block sizes 6 and 8; a 21-qubit block-10 instance is verified in simulation and submitted to hardware. We further provide a breadth-first benchmark of five genuine quantum attacks spanning four symmetric-cipher design paradigms -- Bernstein-Vazirani (linear structure, single query), Grover (SPN key search, quadratic), and Simon (Even-Mansour, CBC-MAC forgery, and Feistel; exponential-to-polynomial in query complexity) -- validated to the classical-simulation ceiling of 25 qubits. We are deliberately explicit about scope: these attacks target reduced or structured constructions in the Q2 (quantum-query) model, asymptotically follow the birthday bound and therefore do not constitute quantum advantage over classical collision-finding, do not break full AES/RSA or 16-round DES, and rely on error mitigation rather than fault-tolerant error correction. Our contribution is the real-hardware demonstration at record structure sizes, the breadth of genuine algorithmic coverage across four paradigms, and an honest, reproducible benchmark with public artifacts.

</details>


### [119] [PRISM: Sensitivity-Aware PolynoMial PRuning for EffIcient Neural Network Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18342)
*Sahaj Majavdia,Mahdi Taheri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了结构化剪枝对同态加密（CKKS）下神经网络可靠性的影响，提出了一种可靠性感知的结构化剪枝方法PSAP，该方法综合权重幅值、多项式激活敏感度和旋转代价对滤波器进行评分，在保持高效性的同时大幅降低了模型在比特翻转攻击下的灾难性精度损失，并确定了安全运行的BER边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝虽被广泛用于使神经网络推理在同态加密下可行，但其对模型可靠性的影响此前未被探索。

Method: 提出Polynomial-Sensitivity-Aware Pruning (PSAP)，一种可靠性感知的结构化剪枝方法。PSAP通过权重幅值、多项式激活敏感度和旋转代价三个维度联合评分滤波器，将剪枝集中在容错区域。同时提出自适应混合度数分配方案降低乘法深度。

Result: PSAP剪枝模型将灾难性精度下降（>10 pp）的层数限制在最多2层，而幅值基线为5-14层；在int32比特翻转注入下最坏情况脆弱性降低最多29倍；确定了CKKS加密下安全运行边界在BER~10^{-5}；Halevi-Shoup旋转次数最多减少45.2%；乘法深度从66层降至56层。

Conclusion: PSAP在保持高效性的同时显著提升了CKKS加密神经网络的可靠性，故障关键结构层仅占参数的1.1%，可通过选择性加固以极低开销实现防护。

Abstract: Structured pruning is essential for making neural network inference feasible under homomorphic encryption (HE), yet its impact on model reliability has remained unexplored. This paper presents a systematic reliability characterization of pruned CKKS-encrypted neural networks and introduces Polynomial-Sensitivity-Aware Pruning (PSAP), a structured pruning method that is inherently reliability-aware. PSAP scores filters jointly by weight magnitude, polynomial activation sensitivity, and rotation cost, which concentrates pruning in fault-tolerant regions. Across two architectures, two datasets, two numerical representations, and five bit-error rates (40 full-model and 108 per-layer experiments), PSAP-pruned models limit catastrophic (>10 pp accuracy drop) layers to at most two versus 5--14 for magnitude-pruned baselines, reducing worst-case vulnerability by up to 29 times under int32 bit-flip injection. Direct CKKS encrypted fault injection indicates a safe operating boundary near BER~ 10^{-5}, supporting int32 injection as a conservative reliability proxy. The fault-critical structural layers account for only 1.1% of parameters, enabling selective hardening at minimal overhead. These reliability gains are obtained alongside competitive efficiency: PSAP reduces Halevi--Shoup rotations by up to 45.2\% on ResNet-32, and an adaptive mixed-degree allocation scheme lowers multiplicative depth from 66 to 56 levels, enabling leveled inference without bootstrapping.

</details>


### [120] [HALLMARK: Diagnosing Three Failure Modes in LLM Citation Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18360)
*Patrik Reizinger,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 HALLMARK，一个包含 2,526 条 BibTeX 条目、14 种幻觉类型、三个难度等级和六项诊断子测试的参考文献幻觉基准测试。通过评估多种验证器发现：假阳性率（FPR）而非召回率是决定验证器是否可部署的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生成的学术文献中参考文献幻觉问题日益严重（NeurIPS 2025 已发现 53 篇含幻觉引文的论文），但缺乏统一的基准测试来比较不同验证方法并提供详细的失败诊断。

Method: 构建 HALLMARK 基准（2,526 条 BibTeX 条目，覆盖 14 种幻觉类型、3 个难度等级、6 项诊断子测试，含抗污染保留集），对 DOI 查询基线、零样本 LLM、工具增强型智能体及基于规则的验证器 bibtex-updater 进行系统评估。

Result: 1）智能体查询提高召回率但显著增加假阳性；2）在真实场景中，假阳性率的数量级差异（而非召回率）决定验证结果主要是真阳性还是噪声；3）大多数 LLM 对超出训练截止日期的论文过度标记为幻觉，仅最新模型能保持 FPR 接近分布内水平。

Conclusion: 假阳性率是验证器部署的核心瓶颈，但未检测到的幻觉对科学记录而言仍是代价更高的错误。HALLMARK 通过三种具体失败模式使这一权衡成为可量化评估的对象。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now routinely draft literature reviews and assist with academic writing, which means a higher risk of fabricated references: GPTZero found 53 papers with hallucinated citations among NeurIPS 2025's accepted set. Rule- and LLM-based verifiers are emerging, but no shared benchmark compares them and gives detailed failure diagnostics. We close that gap with HALLMARK (Hallucination benchmark): 2,526 BibTeX entries spanning 14 hallucination types, three difficulty tiers, six diagnostic sub-tests per entry, and a contamination-resistant held-out split. On it we evaluate a DOI-lookup baseline, frontier LLMs zero-shot, tool-augmented agents, and our own rule-based, co-designed verifier bibtex-updater. Across the benchmark one result is consistent: the false-positive rate, not recall, decides whether a verifier is deployable. HALLMARK makes it concrete through three failure modes: agentic lookups buy recall but inflate false positives; at a venue-realistic base rate, the order-of-magnitude spread in false-positive rates (FPRs) -- not recall -- governs whether a verifier's flags are mostly true catches or mostly noise; and most LLMs over-flag papers published past their training cutoff, where only the two latest-cutoff models hold their false-positive rate near in-distribution levels (a signal we report as descriptive, since it is confounded with possible recall of those entries). Thus FPR is the deployment bottleneck, but an undetected fabrication remains the costlier error for the scientific record.

</details>


### [121] [Adversarial Robustness of Phishing Email Detection: A Comparative Study of TF-IDF + Logistic Regression and Fine-Tuned DistilBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18429)
*Tanveer Ahmed,Seyedali Pourmoafil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对比了TF-IDF+逻辑回归和DistilBERT两种钓鱼邮件检测方法，发现两者在干净数据上准确率超98%，但在对抗性测试中分别降至64.00%和63.64%，性能下降约35个百分点，表明干净数据准确率不能预测对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多数钓鱼邮件检测研究仅在干净、同分布的测试数据上报告准确率，忽视了攻击者故意篡改邮件以逃避检测的对抗性场景，导致模型在实际部署中的鲁棒性被高估。

Method: 使用6个公开数据集的82,255封邮件构建统一语料库，对比TF-IDF+逻辑回归和微调DistilBERT两种方法，在正常同分布、合成钓鱼和对抗性钓鱼三种条件下进行评估，并利用LIME、SHAP和注意力展开分析进行可解释性分析。

Result: 干净数据上两种模型准确率均超98%；对抗测试中TF-IDF+LR降至64.00%（下降34.59个百分点），DistilBERT降至63.64%（下降35.40个百分点）。两种模型在54.9%的对抗样本上预测一致，但各有24和25个独有错误，显示部分互补的失败模式。

Conclusion: 干净数据准确率不能预测对抗鲁棒性，对抗性测试应成为钓鱼邮件检测评估的标准环节。

Abstract: Phishing emails remain one of the most persistent cybersecurity threats, and machine-learning classifiers are widely used to detect them. Most reported detection accuracies, however, are measured on clean, in-distribution test data rather than on emails deliberately altered to evade detection. This paper reports a controlled, pairwise comparison of two phishing-detection approaches a TF-IDF + Logistic Regression baseline and a fine-tuned DistilBERT transformer trained on a unified corpus of 82,255 emails drawn from six public datasets and evaluated under three conditions: normal in-distribution, synthetic phishing, and adversarial phishing. Both models exceeded 98% accuracy on clean data yet degraded sharply under adversarial testing: TF-IDF + LR fell to 64.00% (a 34.59-percentage-point drop) and DistilBERT fell to 63.64% (a 35.40-percentage-point drop) a gap of only 0.36 percentage points, equivalent to a single email in the 275-sample adversarial test set. LIME, SHAP, and attention-rollout analysis indicate the two models relied on different evidence yet showed similar vulnerability. Pairwise error analysis shows the models agreed on 54.9% of adversarial samples but each made a similar number of exclusive errors (24 and 25 respectively), indicating partly complementary rather than identical failure modes. The results show that clean-data accuracy does not predict adversarial robustness, and that adversarial testing should be a standard part of phishing-detection evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [ChainMark: Model-Free LLM Watermarking with Closed-Form Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18445)
*Chengheng Li-Chen,Kyuhee Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ChainMark提出了一种基于马尔可夫链的主动水印方法，通过密钥SHA-256划分词汇表为S个状态，并对部分位置强制硬马尔可夫转移。该方法无需语言模型访问，推导了闭合形式的状态数公式、通用鲁棒性阈值（约29.3%）及其对k-正则拓扑的泛化。实验表明在翻译和随机替换攻击下优于KGW和SWEET，且通过单语料库经验校准可恢复1%的目标误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印检测器依赖生成语言模型和启发式阈值，缺乏闭合形式的校准方法；而监管要求（如欧盟AI法案）需要机器可读的合成文本标记，亟需一种高效、可校准且无需LM访问的水印方案。

Method: 提出ChainMark主动水印方法：(1) 通过密钥SHA-256将词汇表划分为S个状态；(2) 对比例rho的位置强制硬马尔可夫转移；(3) 检测器仅需O(n)次哈希操作回放分区，无需LM访问。推导了最小状态数S*的闭合形式（定理1）、通用鲁棒性阈值δ*≈29.3%（定理2）及其对k-正则拓扑的泛化（定理3）。

Result: 在三个指令微调LLM和四个领域上，ChainMark在翻译和随机替换攻击下严格优于KGW和SWEET；通过单语料库经验校准可恢复1%的目标误报率。

Conclusion: ChainMark是一种高效、可校准、强鲁棒且无需LM访问的合成文本水印方法，满足监管需求，理论上证明了其鲁棒性阈值的不变性。

Abstract: Regulatory regimes such as the EU AI Act mandate machine-readable marking of synthetic text, but existing watermark detectors rely on the generating LM and on heuristic thresholds with no closed-form calibration. We introduce ChainMark, an active watermark that partitions the vocabulary into S states via keyed SHA-256 and forces a hard Markov transition on a fraction rho of positions; the detector replays the partition from the same key in O(n) hash operations, with no LM access. We derive a closed-form S*(n, rho, alpha) mapping a target FPR, text length, and budget to the minimum state count (Theorem 1), prove a universal robustness threshold delta* = 1 - 1/sqrt(2) approximately 29.3% that is invariant in (S, rho, n) (Theorem 2), and generalise both to any k-regular transition topology (Theorem 3). Across three instruction-tuned LLMs and four domains, ChainMark strictly dominates KGW and SWEET under translation and random-substitution attacks at matched budget; a one-corpus empirical recalibration restores the 1% target FPR on natural-language text.

</details>


### [123] [0-Cyclic Equalizability of Binary Words Characterized by Hamming Weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18452)
*Sarunyu Thongjarast*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究二进制卡片序列的0-循环可等化问题：两个等长二进制词当且仅当具有相同汉明权重（即相同数量的1）时，可以通过在匹配位置仅插入0使得它们互为循环移位。论文通过四字母编码和显式构造给出了证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在牌密码学中，随机切牌是最基本的洗牌操作之一，它使得两个牌序列不可区分当且仅当它们互为循环移位。先前研究表明通过插入任意牌可使任意排列等化，本文进一步探究更严格的约束：在仅能插入0的二进制牌场景下，两个词能否变得不可区分。

Method: 将一对二进制词编码为四字母字母表{A, B, X, O}上的单个词，将0-循环可等化条件简化为该编码下的更简单条件，并显式构造出所需的插入方案。

Result: 两个等长二进制词是0-循环可等化的当且仅当它们具有相同的汉明权重（即相同数量的1）。必要性显然成立，充分性通过构造性证明得到。

Conclusion: 本文解决了二进制卡片场景下的0-循环可等化问题，证明了汉明权重相等是充要条件，为牌密码学中的序列不可区分性提供了更强的理论基础。

Abstract: The random cut is one of the most fundamental shuffles in card-based cryptography: it rotates a sequence of face-down cards by a secret amount. Under this shuffle, two sequences of cards are indistinguishable if and only if they are cyclic shifts of each other. This motivates the question of whether, given two sequences of cards, inserting cards at matching positions can make them indistinguishable. A previous study shows that such an insertion is always possible when any cards may be inserted, as long as the two words are permutations of each other. This paper considers a stronger restriction: if the cards are binary, carrying only 0 or 1, can we insert only 0s to make the sequences indistinguishable? We call two words 0-cyclically equalizable if one can insert 0s into both sequences at matching positions so that the resulting words are cyclic shifts of each other. Our main result is that two binary words of equal length are 0-cyclically equalizable if and only if they have equal Hamming weight, that is, the same number of 1-bits. Since equal Hamming weight is clearly necessary, the content of the paper is to show that it is also sufficient. Our proof is constructive: we encode a pair of binary words as a single word over the four-letter alphabet {A, B, X, O}, reduce equalizability to a simpler condition in this encoding, and build the required insertion explicitly.

</details>


### [124] [Trusted Credentials, Untrusted Behavior: Benchmarking LLM-Agent Security in High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18485)
*Jie Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义了高性能计算（HPC）环境中LLM智能体面临的“授权智能体劫持”问题：攻击者通过日志、工具描述、共享文件或智能体间消息中的对抗性指令，将已授权智能体重定向到非用户本意的任务，现有账户级安全控制无法防御。论文提出了HPC场景下的威胁模型，识别了调度器、共享存储、多项目账户和科学工作流中的攻击面，并规划了任务边界基准TaskBound。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC安全机制（身份认证、隔离）无法捕获“授权但被劫持的智能体”这一失败模式——智能体持有用户凭证和权限，但被对抗性输入诱导执行非用户意图的操作，而每个命令从账户层面看都是合法的。

Method: 1. 定义HPC场景下授权智能体劫持的威胁模型；2. 识别调度器、共享存储、多项目账户、科学工作流等HPC特有攻击面；3. 分析现有安全控制的不足；4. 提出研究议程和TaskBound基准计划。

Result: 明确了HPC中LLM智能体的新型安全威胁（授权智能体劫持），指出间接提示注入和工具滥用等机制在HPC特有环境（调度器、共享文件系统、多项目账户）中的攻击面，并指出现有账户级访问控制无法解决该问题。

Conclusion: HPC需要一种能表示任务意图的新安全范式，以补充现有的身份和隔离控制。论文提出了研究议程和TaskBound基准作为后续工作的路线图。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are starting to take on routine work in high-performance computing (HPC), including monitoring Slurm jobs, diagnosing failed builds, inspecting simulation output, and coordinating scientific workflows. To do this work, an agent commonly acts under its user's credentials and inherits the user's access to files and the scheduler. This arrangement creates a failure mode that ordinary account-level controls do not capture. Adversarial instructions in a log, tool description, shared file, or peer-agent message may redirect the agent beyond the task the user assigned, even though every resulting command is authenticated and permitted for that account. We refer to this as the hijacked authorized agent problem. Existing agent-security studies explain relevant mechanisms, such as indirect prompt injection and tool misuse, but generally evaluate them in web, enterprise, or personal-assistant settings. HPC security, by contrast, has mature controls for identity and isolation but does not ordinarily represent the intent of a particular task. This paper defines the threat model in the HPC setting, identifies attack surfaces created by schedulers, shared storage, multi-project accounts, and scientific workflows, and examines where current controls fall short. It concludes with a research agenda and a plan for an empirical benchmark, TaskBound.

</details>


### [125] [Towards an Automated Test of LLM Security Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18496)
*Shufan Chai,Liangliang Sun,Jessica Staddon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种半自动化方法，利用消费者保护机构的权威信息评估LLM在安全领域的知识缺口，并通过身份盗窃和冒名诈骗两个主题在Gemini和GPT系列5个模型上验证了该方法能有效区分模型是否具备足够的安全知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM安全知识的方法（如构建挑战问题集或任务基准）需要大量人工工作和安全专业知识，效率低下且成本高。

Method: 利用消费者保护机构（CPAs）的权威信息作为参考标准，通过分析LLM对安全主题应答的稳定性（即知识缺口导致的不一致性）来评估模型知识掌握程度，方法部分自动化。

Result: 在身份盗窃和冒名诈骗两个安全主题上，使用6个CPAs的公开信息，对Gemini和GPT两个家族的5个LLM进行测试，该方法能有效区分具备与不具备足够安全知识的模型。

Conclusion: 提出的半自动化方法可行且有效，能够识别LLM在特定安全领域的知识缺口，减少人工评估成本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for a range of software, hardware and human-centered security tasks. Consequently, LLM performance on security tasks is an active area of measurement and research, often with a focus on identifying areas in which LLM security ``knowledge'' may be insufficient. Popular strategies for identifying LLM security knowledge gaps include building corpora of challenge questions or task benchmarks, strategies that require substantial manual work and security expertise to design and execute. We introduce a partially-automated method for assessing LLM knowledge of a security area.The method uses authoritative information from Consumer Protection Agencies (CPAs) to identify instability in LLM responses that can be indicative of knowledge gaps. We demonstrate the method for 2 security topics, identity theft and impostor scams, and 5 LLMs in 2 leading LLM families, Gemini and GPT, using publicly available information about identity theft and impostor scams from 6 CPAs.The method distinguishes between models that have and don't have sufficient knowledge to accurately identify the security topics in text narratives.

</details>


### [126] [CryptanalysisBench: Can LLMs do Cryptanalysis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18538)
*Lukas Fluri,Avital Shafran,Nicholas Carlini,Matthew Jagielski,Milad Nasr,Orr Dunkelman,Eyal Ronen,Florian Tramèr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CryptanalysisBench，一个包含 191 个密码分析任务的基准测试，证明前沿 LLM（如 Claude Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5 等）能破解 65%-86% 的 Tier-1 方案，并发现未知的新型攻击（如 SpoC 的密钥恢复攻击和 KINDI 的 CCA 安全性证明错误），表明 AI 密码分析正接近甚至超越现有技术水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码分析位于数学推理与网络安全的交叉点，是测试 LLM 推理能力的理想场景，且密码原语的安全性对数字安全至关重要。作者希望探究 LLM 是否能执行密码分析，并为此建立系统化的评估基准。

Method: 构建 CryptanalysisBench，包含 191 个任务，涵盖六类密码原语（分组密码、哈希函数等），主要来自四个 NIST 标准化竞赛。基准分为三个层级：已知实际破解的原语、无已知实际破解的原语（含全强度和缩尺变体）、以及前沿生产级原语。使用五个前沿 LLM 进行测试。

Result: 五个模型破解了 65%-86% 的 Tier-1 方案，6-12 个全强度 Tier-2 方案，以及 24-61 个缩尺变体。模型还发现了新型密码分析，包括 SpoC AEAD 的设计缺陷密钥恢复攻击和 KINDI 的 CCA 安全性证明错误，此前均未见报道。

Conclusion: LLM 在密码分析方面的能力正在快速提升，可能很快（在某些方面已）超越已发表的现有技术水平。CryptanalysisBench 可作为追踪 AI 密码分析进展的工具，并用于候选方案的压力测试。

Abstract: Cryptanalysis - the task of finding attacks against cryptographic schemes - sits at the intersection of mathematical reasoning and cybersecurity, two areas where LLMs have advanced fastest. Cryptanalysis represents both a clean testbed for frontier reasoning (as practical attacks can be automatically verified) and a domain with unusually high stakes, since the primitives under study underpin our digital security. In this paper we ask whether LLMs can do cryptanalysis, and find that the answer is increasingly yes.
  We introduce CryptanalysisBench, 191 tasks across six families of cryptographic primitives (block ciphers, hash functions, etc.) drawn primarily from four NIST standardization competitions. Our benchmark consists of three tiers: (i) primitives with known practical breaks; (ii) primitives with no known practical break, evaluated both at full strength and as scaled-down variants; and (iii) a challenge set of production primitives at the frontier of cryptanalysis.
  Five frontier models (Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 5, Mythos 5, GPT 5.5, and the open-weights GLM 5.2) break 65%-86% of Tier 1 schemes, 6-12 Tier-2 schemes at full strength, and 24-61 across all scaled-down variants. Beyond deriving known results, models produce novel cryptanalysis, such as a key-recovery attack that exploits a design flaw in the SpoC AEAD and an error in KINDI's published CCA-security proof, both to the best of our knowledge not previously known.
  We release CryptanalysisBench as a tool to help track if (or when) AI cryptanalysis becomes a serious factor and as a scaffold for stress-testing candidate schemes before deployment. The attacks that the benchmark already surfaces are an early snapshot of a fast-moving frontier that may soon match, and in places exceed, the published state of the art.

</details>


### [127] [RECEIPT: Deterministic, Reward-Hacking-Resistant Verification for White-Box Agentic XSS Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18575)
*Muxi Lyu,Karen Shieh,Yiwei Hou,Hao Wang,Koushik Sen,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出RECEIPT验证框架，通过环境隔离、PoC约束、角色分离和判决绑定，使LLM驱动的代码代理发现的XSS漏洞可信，在95个真实应用中发现了24个未知XSS漏洞，零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代码代理在发现XSS漏洞时虽然有效，但其自我报告的结果不可信，存在奖励篡改行为（reward-hacking），需要可信的验证机制来确认漏洞的真实性。

Method: RECEIPT框架通过四项关键约束确保验证的可信度：环境隔离（确保脚本在真实浏览器中运行）、PoC约束（验证payload由攻击者植入并在受害者浏览器中执行）、角色分离（区分攻击者和受害者角色）、判决绑定（使验证过程确定且可复现）。

Result: 在95个真实应用上，以每应用$20预算，发现24个未知XSS漏洞（其中12个已获维护者确认），在已知漏洞恢复目标中36%复现了标记的CVE，相比自我判断和黑盒扫描器，确认了更多真实漏洞且零误报。

Conclusion: RECEIPT框架有效解决了LLM代理在XSS发现中报告结果不可信的问题，通过约束性重放验证实现了确定性、可复现的漏洞确认，并能以低成本发现真实世界中的未知XSS漏洞。

Abstract: Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) remains one of the most prevalent and damaging classes of web vulnerabilities. LLM-based coding agents offer a promising approach to XSS discovery by combining source-code reasoning with interactive testing against a running application. However, a coding agent's claims cannot be trusted on their own. We characterize three reward-hacking behaviors in white-box agentic XSS discovery and propose three requirements that an ideal verifier should meet.
  We present RECEIPT, a verification framework that makes agent-reported XSS findings trustworthy by enforcing environment isolation, PoC constraints, role separation, and verdict binding. Each confirmation therefore establishes two properties: the script runs in a real browser, and the payload was planted under the attacker role and executed in the victim role's browser. This constrained replay procedure makes validation deterministic and reproducible. We evaluate RECEIPT on 95 real-world web-application targets drawn from popular open-source projects. Within a $20 per-application budget, RECEIPT found 24 previously unknown XSS vulnerabilities, 12 of which have already been acknowledged by maintainers after responsible disclosure, and recovered the labeled CVE in 36% of known-vulnerability recovery targets. Compared with the same agent using self-judgment and with black-box scanners, RECEIPT confirms more real exploits while admitting no false positives.

</details>


### [128] [CPInj: Uncovering Prompt Injection Risks in Textual Collaborative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18622)
*Xinting Liao,Behnoosh Zamanlooy,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,David B. Emerson,Ruinan Jin,Deval Pandya,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CPInj攻击，揭示了文本协作提示优化（TCPO）在去中心化LLM提示优化中存在的新型注入攻击面，恶意指令可通过协作优化循环传播并逃避现有防御；同时提出防御性聚合方法APAgg进行初步缓解，但攻击仍高度有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCPO将Textgrad扩展到去中心化场景，允许多客户端协作优化LLM提示而无需共享本地数据。但其基于自由文本的更新与聚合机制引入了新的攻击面——恶意指令可被注入到本地提示中并通过服务器端聚合传播。现有研究尚未探索这一风险，因此需要揭示TCPO在协作优化循环中的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出CPInj（协作提示注入攻击），通过污染聚合后的全局提示来植入恶意指令，目标是使恶意指令：1）在聚合后存活；2）在后续良性客户端的提示优化中持续存在；3）规避服务器端基于检测的防御。同时提出防御性聚合方法APAgg，通过净化恶意指令来部分恢复TCPO的效用。

Result: 在三个LLM家族和五个推理任务（数学、逻辑、医学）上的实验表明：CPInj能有效攻击TCPO，现有防御方法对其无效；APAgg虽能部分缓解，但攻击仍然高度有效，问题远未彻底解决。

Conclusion: TCPO存在关键的安全漏洞，CPInj攻击揭示了这一风险；当前的防御手段不足以应对此类攻击，亟需更强健的防御机制来保护TCPO。

Abstract: Textual Collaborative Prompt Optimization (TCPO) extends Textgrad (Yuksekgonul et al., 2025) to a decentralized setting by allowing multiple clients to jointly improve prompts for large language models (LLMs) while keeping their data locally. Its reliance on free-form textual updating and aggregation introduces a new and largely unexplored attack surface, i.e., malicious instructions can be injected into local prompts and propagated through server-side prompt aggregation. Unlike conventional prompt injection attacks, attacking TCPO targets the collaborative optimization loop in TCPO. This setting is more challenging because malicious instructions must survive aggregation, persist through subsequent benign prompt optimization, and evade server-side defenses. To expose this risk, we propose CPInj, a collaborative prompt injection attack that contaminates the aggregated global prompt with malicious instructions, degrades downstream task performance, resists purification by prompt optimization on benign clients, and evades advanced detection-based defenses on the server. We find that current defense methods are ineffective against CPInj. To mitigate this attack, we further propose a defense-oriented aggregation method, i.e., APAgg, which purifies malicious instructions and partially recovers TCPO utility. We conduct extensive experiments across three LLM families and five reasoning tasks in math, logic, and medicine. The results demonstrate that our proposed attack reveals a critical vulnerability in TCPO. Although we take a first step toward mitigation, the attack remains highly effective and far from fully resolved, calling for more robust defense for TCPO.

</details>


### [129] [Broken Gates: Re-evaluating Web Bot Defenses in the Age of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18659)
*Behzad Ousat,Nikita Turkmen,Lalchandra Rampersaud,Dillan Bailey,Amin Kharraz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于挑战的交互式防御和非交互式信任防御两类机器人管理系统对LLM浏览器代理和商业打码服务的抵抗力。研究发现：挑战式防御对商业打码服务几乎无效，也能被带有专用求解模块的LLM代理破解；非交互式防御（如reCaptcha v3）看似更强，但其安全性源于执行环境真实性而非代理行为，存在根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的浏览器代理能够自主导航、推理页面内容并用自然语言与网页交互，这种进化对广泛部署的机器人管理系统的有效性提出了根本性质疑。

Method: 对7家打码服务和6种LLM代理（涵盖云端、自托管、AI辅助和浏览器扩展配置）进行系统测量，测试对象包括hCaptcha、reCaptcha v2、reCaptcha v3和Cloudflare Turnstile，并通过细粒度交互轨迹分析探究防御失效的根因。

Result: 挑战式防御被商业打码服务以近乎完美的绕过率和极低成本突破；LLM代理在配备专用求解模块时也能击败挑战；非交互式防御（reCaptcha v3）看似更强，但分析发现行为足迹几乎相同的两个代理结果截然不同——决定性因素是执行环境真实性而非代理行为。

Conclusion: 非交互式防御的安全边界位于环境层而非行为层，这对机器人管理系统的设计和评估具有重要启示。

Abstract: LLM-based browser agents are rapidly changing the threat landscape for web security. Unlike traditional automation frameworks that execute predefined scripts, these agents can autonomously navigate websites, reason about page content, and interact with web interfaces using natural-language instructions. This evolution raises fundamental questions about the effectiveness of bot management systems, widely deployed to defend against automated web abuse. In this paper, we present a systematic measurement study evaluating the resilience of both interactive challenge-based defenses and non-interactive trust-based defenses against two attacker classes: commercial Captcha-solving services and LLM-based browser agents. Our evaluation spans seven solver services and six agents, including cloud-hosted, self-hosted, AI-assisted, and browser-extension configurations, tested against hCaptcha, reCaptcha v2, reCaptcha v3, and Cloudflare Turnstile. Our results show that challenge-based defenses are broadly ineffective against commercial solvers, which achieve near-perfect bypass at negligible cost. The challenges can similarly be defeated by LLM-based agents when a dedicated solver module is available. Non-interactive defenses such as reCaptcha v3 exhibit stronger resistance, but our analysis reveals that this resilience does not reflect a fundamental security property. Through fine-grained interaction trace analysis, we find that two agents with nearly indistinguishable behavioral footprints yield divergent outcomes, one bypassing the defense and one failing, isolating execution-environment authenticity, rather than agent behavior, as the determining factor. These findings suggest that the security boundary of non-interactive defenses lies at the environment layer, with significant implications for how bot management systems are designed and evaluated.

</details>


### [130] [When to Trust the Map: Confidence-Aware LLM Routing for Automotive CVE-to-ATM Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18684)
*Heeyun Heo,Sangmin Park,Huy Kang Kim,Sanghoon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将CVE描述自动映射到Auto-ISAC汽车威胁矩阵(ATM)的选择性自动化框架，通过分层上下文学习生成候选映射，融合自一致性与LLM证据验证信号构建校准元模型，将映射路由至AUTO、REVIEW或HOLD三类，在高置信度模式下AUTO层精确率达0.878，AUROC达0.868。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVE描述与Auto-ISAC ATM表示方式不对齐，直接自动映射可能导致安全关键环境中的威胁解读和缓解优先级失真，需要一种置信度感知方法来区分可自动确认的映射与不确定情况。

Method: 将CVE到ATM映射重构为选择性自动化问题；采用分层上下文学习生成候选映射；融合自一致性信号与LLM证据验证信号；构建校准的元模型输出置信度分数；根据置信度将候选映射路由至AUTO(自动)、REVIEW(审查)或HOLD(搁置)三个层级。

Result: 在评估集上，相比Flat零样本GPT-5.2基线，候选集精度在匹配召回率下显著提升；高置信度模式下AUTO层精度达0.878，是候选集基率的两倍以上；校准置信度分数区分正确与错误候选的AUROC达0.868。

Conclusion: 该框架能够通过分离可自动确认的映射与需要分析师审查的映射来支持选择性自动化，有效提升安全关键环境中威胁映射的可靠性。

Abstract: Public CVE descriptions report the technical conditions and impact of vulnerabilities, whereas the Auto-ISAC Automotive Threat Matrix (ATM) expresses an adversary's tactics and techniques. Because the two representations are not directly aligned, incorrect automated mappings in safety-critical environments may distort threat interpretation and mitigation prioritization, motivating a confidence-aware approach that distinguishes auto-confirmable mappings from uncertain cases. This paper reformulates automotive CVE-to-ATM mapping as a selective automation problem. The proposed framework generates candidate mappings via hierarchical in-context learning, then fuses self-consistency and LLM-based evidence verification signals into a calibrated meta-model. The resulting calibrated confidence score routes each candidate into AUTO, REVIEW, or HOLD. On the evaluation set, the proposed system substantially improved candidate-set precision at matched recall over a Flat zero-shot GPT-5.2 baseline. In the High-Confidence operating mode, the AUTO tier achieved a precision of 0.878, more than double the candidate-set base rate, and the calibrated confidence score achieved an AUROC of 0.868 in distinguishing correct from incorrect candidates. These results show that the framework can support selective automation by isolating auto-confirmable mappings from those requiring analyst review.

</details>


### [131] [GLID: Gated Local Intrinsic Dimension Repairs the Blind Spots of Face-Forgery Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18770)
*Guang Yang,Fengchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GLID通过估计冻结ViT中图像块token的局部内在维度（LID），构建了一种无需训练的12维几何信号，并利用置信门控机制将其注入微调检测器，在跨生成器基准上显著提升对未见生成器家族的检测能力（平均AUC 0.805），同时揭示了伪造人脸弯曲token流形的两个经验定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调基础模型检测器在跨生成器家族时存在盲区，即对训练中未见过的生成器生成的伪造人脸检测失效。作者希望通过几何信号而非大量数据来修复这一盲区。

Method: GLID方法包括：(1) 从单张图像的冻结Vision Transformer多个深度层中提取patch token，估计其局部内在维度（LID），形成12维几何信号；(2) 通过置信门控（confidence gate）将该信号注入微调检测器，门的强度仅在分布内数据上校准，无需在未见生成器上训练。

Result: 在16轴跨生成器基准上，GLID达到0.805平均AUC，超越所有重训练的基线模型。在生成轴上提升+0.084 AUC，在reenactment轴上仅下降-0.005 AUC。跨种子准确率标准差降低5.5倍。

Conclusion: 发现两个经验定律：(1) 伪造人脸在特定深度弯曲token流形——GAN伪影集中在最后一层，扩散伪影集中在中间层，该模式跨4种骨干网络、3种维度估计器和非人脸图像均成立；(2) 微调仅在训练数据覆盖处吸收辅助增益——注入1%目标家族图像会消除+0.100的增益，因此几何信号在数据不可用处至关重要。

Abstract: Fine-tuned foundation-model detectors dominate face-forgery benchmarks, yet they stay blind to generator families absent from training. We present GLID, a detector that repairs this blind spot with geometry instead of data. GLID treats the patch tokens of a single image as a sample from a manifold and estimates their local intrinsic dimension (LID) at several depths of a frozen vision transformer. This 12-dimensional, training-free signal enters a fine-tuned detector through a confidence gate whose strength is calibrated purely in-distribution. On a 16-axis cross-generator benchmark, GLID reaches 0.805 mean AUC, first among retrained state-of-the-art baselines and never significantly behind the strongest of them on any axis. It lifts the generation axes by +0.084 AUC while moving reenactment by only -0.005. Two empirical laws explain the design. First, forged faces bend the token manifold at family-specific depths: GAN artifacts peak at the last layer, diffusion artifacts peak mid-network, and the pattern survives four backbones, three dimension estimators, and non-face imagery. Second, fine-tuning absorbs auxiliary gains exactly where training data covers: injecting 1% target-family images erases a +0.100 gain, so geometric signals matter precisely where data is unavailable. The deterministic signal also cuts the cross-seed spread of accuracy 5.5x. Code, preregistered analysis gates, and per-image scores accompany the paper.

</details>


### [132] [Cross-Agent Campaign Attribution: Linking Asynchronous Attacks Across LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18826)
*SangJin Park,Myungsub Choi,Jineok Kim,Minseung Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨智能体异步战役归因问题，并设计了A²FV指纹向量协议，用于在无共享运行时状态下关联同一潜在对抗战役的会话。实验表明该方法在SCD-v1基准上达到0.82的成对AUC，显著优于基线方法，为LLM智能体部署提供了新的安全评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体防御通常按单次会话独立评估，但在实际部署中攻击可跨多个独立智能体、团队和运行时分布，导致每个局部防护仅能观测到稀疏的攻击片段。这种碎片化观测缺乏跨会话关联能力，无法识别属于同一潜在对抗战役的攻击活动。

Method: 提出异步归因指纹向量(A²FV)协议，这是一种轻量级代理端参考协议，通过代理可观测的工具使用模式、时间特征和提示残留特征来评分成对战役相似度。同时构建了SCD-v1受控基准数据集，包含良性流量、隔离攻击、多会话战役、匹配的非Oracle逃避和泄漏审计。

Result: 在SCD-v1基准上，A²FV在战役关联任务上达到0.82的成对AUC，而基于分数的单会话检测器和分块LLM评判器在相同任务上接近随机水平。结构性和风格测量残留携带最强的固定信号，时间特征作为更丰富代理轨迹的诊断通道。

Conclusion: 跨智能体战役归因应作为保护LLM智能体实际部署的一个独立评估层。交叉风格控制表明信号部分风格敏感但不可简化为单一风格，静态和维度感知的非Oracle压力测试进一步证明在受控逃避下成对可分性仍然存在。

Abstract: LLM-agent defenses are typically evaluated one session at a time. In deployment, however, attacks can be distributed across independent agents, teams, and runtimes, leaving each local guardrail with only a sparse fragment. We formalize cross-agent asynchronous campaign attribution: linking sessions from the same latent adversarial campaign without shared runtime state, test-time campaign labels, or attacker identity oracles. We introduce Asynchronous Attribution Fingerprint Vectors ($A^2FV$), a lightweight proxy-side reference protocol for scoring pairwise campaign similarity from proxy-observable tool-use, timing, and prompt residue. We also construct SCD-v1, a controlled persona-matched benchmark with benign traffic, isolated attacks, multi-session campaigns, matched non-oracle evasion, and leakage audits. On SCD-v1, $A^2FV$ achieves 0.82 pairwise AUC for campaign linking, while score-only adaptations of per-session detectors and chunked LLM judges remain near chance under the same task. The strongest fixed signal is carried by structural and stylometric residue, while timing is retained as a diagnostic channel for richer proxy traces. Crossed-style controls show that the signal is partly style-sensitive but not reducible to style alone. Static and dimension-aware non-oracle stress tests further show that pairwise separability persists under controlled evasion. These results establish cross-agent campaign attribution as a distinct evaluation layer for securing LLM agents in the wild.

</details>


### [133] [Data Leakage Prevention in Agentic Applications via Preemptive Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18847)
*Akansha Shukla,Emily Bellov,Parth Atulbhai Gandhi,Yuval Elovici,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体系统的预部署扫描、加固和验证流水线，用于检测和修复指令/数据边界失效及提示注入攻击导致的数据泄露和工具滥用问题。该流水线通过分析提示模板、工具接口和调用代码，识别泄露模式并生成最小化补丁，再通过对抗性攻击和良性输入变体进行验证。在五个真实应用和AgentDojo基准测试中，该方法在基本攻击下实现100%的泄露消除，在压力诱导操纵下减少91%的泄露，且无需运行时策略持续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统将LLM驱动的规划与外部工具接口集成，使得数据泄露和工具滥用通过指令/数据边界失效和提示注入攻击成为可能。在跨多个代码库和异构代理的工作流中，强制执行所需控制尤其具有挑战性。现有方法缺乏针对多智能体系统的预部署安全流水线。

Method: 提出预部署流水线，包含三个核心阶段：1）扫描阶段：分析提示模板、工具接口和工具调用代码，识别泄露模式；2）加固阶段：优先处理高风险工具，应用最小侵入性缓解措施，包括模式收紧、边界清理、基于允许列表的工具门控和最小权限检查；3）验证阶段：自动生成模仿越狱、指令覆盖和工具目标操纵的攻击输入，以及良性任务变体，确认加固后功能正常。

Result: 在五个真实世界智能体应用和AgentDojo基准测试中评估。流水线识别出重复出现的泄露模式，生成的补丁在不破坏预期行为的前提下可集成。在基本越狱和指令覆盖攻击下实现100%泄露消除，在压力诱导操纵下减少91%泄露，无需持续运行时策略执行。

Conclusion: 所提出的预部署流水线能有效解决多智能体系统中的安全挑战，通过扫描、加固和验证三个阶段的组合，在不影响应用功能的前提下显著降低数据泄露和工具滥用风险，为智能体系统的安全部署提供了一种实用的预部署解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems integrate LLM driven planning with interfaces to external tools, making data leakage and tool misuse feasible via instruction/data boundary failures and prompt injection attacks. Enforcing required controls consistently is particularly challenging in workflows spanning many codebases and heterogeneous agents. To address this challenge in multi agentic systems, we present a pre-deployment pipeline for scanning, hardening, and validation of agentic applications. The pipeline analyzes prompt templates, tool interfaces, and tool-invocation code to identify leakage-enabling patterns and generate actionable patches. The hardened application is then validated through adversarial prompt injection attacks and benign input variations ensuring that mitigations do not disrupt intended behavior. In the hardening stage, high-risk tools are prioritized, and minimally invasive mitigations are applied, including schema tightening, boundary sanitization, allowlist-based tool gating, and least-privilege checks. In the validation stage, the pipeline automatically generates attack inputs that mimic jailbreaks, instruction overrides, and tool-targeted manipulation, along with benign task variants, to confirm that the functionality of the hardened application is preserved after remediation. We evaluated the pipeline on five real-world agentic applications, as well as on the AgentDojo benchmark. Across all applications, the proposed pipeline identified recurring leakage-enabling patterns and generated patches that can be integrated without disrupting the intended application behavior.
